# Daily video prompt pack — 30/10/2026 · Ox day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ)

Lucky signs: Ox, Snake, Rooster · Careful: Goat
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.

Voice-over wording: standard script (this pack was made ahead; the site's own daily wording appears the day before).

## Luckiest animal today – Ox (ឆ្លូវ) · Great Day
One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color white (ស) · best time 09:00–11:00 · lucky numbers 4 · 14 · 445 · 1401 · 11491 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a premium-quality 3D animated fantasy character of a strong, adorable golden ox with big gentle eyes, polished curved horns and ruby studs on its harness, the Ox of the Chinese zodiac, inspired by the charm and enchanting visual appeal of classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films. The ox symbolizes hard work, patience and honest strength: a calm, steady and strong expression and pose that show this. Body in creamy white-gold with shining engraved gold patterns, pink inner ears or rosy cheeks, polished gold textures, luminous highlights and delicate pink and gold accents. It sits on a round golden pedestal inside a large glowing pink lotus floating on a still pond. Behind it, a huge glowing full moon with a soft silver-gold halo, a deep indigo starry sky, a softly illuminated Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with warm golden lights, palm tree silhouettes, small floating lanterns, glowing lotus flowers on the water, gentle moonbeams falling on the ox, magical sparkles and soft silver-and-gold reflections. Cinematic lighting, soft atmospheric glow, realistic metallic reflections, beautiful depth of field, professional animated-movie-quality rendering. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around the character. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cropped body parts.

## 60-second video – Facebook & TikTok (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video about the Ox, today's luckiest animal: 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Ox as ingredients. Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Topic and audience
Today's luckiest zodiac animal, the Ox, in 80 seconds. For viewers who follow Chinese zodiac signs, especially people born in the Year of the Ox, scrolling Facebook Reels and TikTok.

1. Identity

★ 2. Curiosity

3. Question
   English: Which zodiac animal is the luckiest today? Here's the answer, and what it means for you.
   Khmer: តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេនៅថ្ងៃនេះ? នេះគឺជាចម្លើយ និងអត្ថន័យសម្រាប់អ្នក។
4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: reliable, honest, and hard-working.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ ទុកចិត្តបាន ស្មោះត្រង់ និងឧស្សាហ៍ព្យាយាម។
5. Fact
   English: Today is an Ox day, and in the twelve-day zodiac cycle, that makes the Ox today's luckiest sign.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ហើយតាមវដ្ដដប់ពីរថ្ងៃ ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ គឺជារាសីដែលមានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video. Why: Specific and true (only the day's animal has a Great Day), creates curiosity, and every viewer wants to know if it is their sign.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Ox, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Ox as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Ox's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Ox and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Ox? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវមែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?

## Voice-over – 180-second video

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Ox? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Friday, 30 October 2026, is an Ox day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Ox. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Steady and strong, the Ox symbolizes hard work, patience, and honest strength. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Ox are reliable, honest, and hard-working. Oxen build success slowly and steadily through persistence.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Ox? Your luck is strong today. Stay calm, steady and strong. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
For love and family today, be warm and kind. A kind word, a smile, or a small gift can bring you closer to the people you love.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
For work and study, take one clear step at a time. Finish what matters most first, and ask for help when you need it.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
For money, keep things simple and careful today. Plan before you spend, and save a little for tomorrow.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is white. Wear something white, or carry a small white item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 9 to 11 in the morning. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the southeast. When you start something new, face the southeast, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 4, 14, and 445. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 4, 14, 445!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Ox's lucky days of the week are Wednesday and Friday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Ox can be stubborn and slow to adapt to sudden change. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: white. Best time: from 9 to 11 in the morning. Lucky direction: southeast. Lucky numbers: 4, 14, and 445.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

00:00:07,500 --> 00:00:09,950
ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។

4
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,276
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃសុក្រ ទី 30 ខែតុលា

5
00:00:12,326 --> 00:00:14,601
ឆ្នាំ 2026 គឺជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ។

6
00:00:14,651 --> 00:00:18,012
នេះជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ។

7
00:00:18,062 --> 00:00:19,950
ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក!

8
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,043
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ នឹងនរ និងរឹងមាំ។

9
00:00:22,093 --> 00:00:24,059
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវតំណាងឲ្យការខិតខំ

10
00:00:24,109 --> 00:00:26,772
ការអត់ធ្មត់ និងកម្លាំងដ៏ស្មោះត្រង់។

11
00:00:26,822 --> 00:00:29,950
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថាមពលល្អទាំងនេះ កំពុងនៅជាមួយអ្នក។

12
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:33,314
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ជាមនុស្សទុកចិត្តបាន

13
00:00:33,364 --> 00:00:35,950
ស្មោះត្រង់ និងឧស្សាហ៍ព្យាយាម។

14
00:00:36,000 --> 00:00:39,950
ឆ្លូវសាងជោគជ័យបន្តិចម្តងៗដោយការតស៊ូមិនឈប់ឈរ។

15
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,286
សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ថ្ងៃនេះ

16
00:00:42,336 --> 00:00:44,257
សំណាងរបស់អ្នកល្អខ្លាំងណាស់។

17
00:00:44,307 --> 00:00:46,884
សូមរក្សាចិត្តឲ្យស្ងប់ ទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ

18
00:00:46,934 --> 00:00:49,950
ហើយចែករំលែកភាពរីករាយ ដល់មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន។

19
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,069
សម្រាប់ស្នេហា និងគ្រួសារ ថ្ងៃនេះ

20
00:00:52,119 --> 00:00:54,056
សូមមានចិត្តកក់ក្ដៅ និងសប្បុរស។

21
00:00:54,106 --> 00:00:56,440
ពាក្យសម្ដីល្អ ស្នាមញញឹម ឬអំណោយតូចមួយ

22
00:00:56,490 --> 00:00:58,361
អាចនាំឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែជិតស្និទ្ធ

23
00:00:58,411 --> 00:00:59,950
នឹងមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់។

24
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,905
សម្រាប់ការងារ និងការសិក្សា

25
00:01:01,955 --> 00:01:04,687
សូមធ្វើម្ដងមួយជំហាន ឲ្យបានច្បាស់លាស់។

26
00:01:04,737 --> 00:01:06,642
សូមធ្វើរឿងសំខាន់បំផុតមុនគេ

27
00:01:06,692 --> 00:01:09,950
ហើយកុំខ្មាសក្នុងការសុំជំនួយ ពេលអ្នកត្រូវការ។

28
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:11,932
សម្រាប់លុយកាក់ ថ្ងៃនេះ

29
00:01:11,982 --> 00:01:15,175
សូមចាយវាយឲ្យសាមញ្ញ និងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។

30
00:01:15,225 --> 00:01:16,797
សូមគិតគូរមុននឹងចាយ

31
00:01:16,847 --> 00:01:19,950
ហើយសន្សំទុកបន្តិច សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃស្អែក។

32
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,902
ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌ស។

33
00:01:22,952 --> 00:01:26,807
សូមស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌ស ឬយករបស់ពណ៌សមួយ ជាប់ខ្លួន

34
00:01:26,857 --> 00:01:29,950
ដើម្បីរក្សាសំណាងល្អ ឲ្យនៅជិតអ្នក។

35
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,335
ពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ

36
00:01:32,385 --> 00:01:34,721
គឺម៉ោង 9 ដល់ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក។

37
00:01:34,771 --> 00:01:37,381
ជាពេលល្អ សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ

38
00:01:37,431 --> 00:01:39,950
ការជួបជុំ ឬការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។

39

ទិសសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

40
00:01:43,040 --> 00:01:44,910

41

សូមបែរមុខទៅទិសអាគ្នេយ៍ ហើយបោះជំហានទៅមុខ

42
00:01:48,080 --> 00:01:49,950
ដោយចិត្តស្ងប់ និងរីករាយ។

43

44
00:01:53,727 --> 00:01:57,314
សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់

45
00:01:57,364 --> 00:01:59,950

46

47
00:02:03,394 --> 00:02:05,455

48
00:02:05,505 --> 00:02:07,198
សូមចងចាំថ្ងៃទាំងនេះ

49

សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ របស់អ្នក។

50

សូមរំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ជួនកាលរឹងរូស

51
00:02:13,239 --> 00:02:16,711
និងយឺតក្នុងការសម្របខ្លួនទៅនឹងការផ្លាស់ប្តូរភ្លាមៗ។

52
00:02:16,761 --> 00:02:18,401
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព

53
00:02:18,451 --> 00:02:19,950
និងស្ដាប់ចិត្តខ្លួនឯង។

54
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:22,514
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ

55
00:02:22,564 --> 00:02:26,446
ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។

56
00:02:26,496 --> 00:02:29,950
ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

57
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:31,306
មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់

58
00:02:31,356 --> 00:02:34,526
សូមចំណាយពេលស្ងាត់មួយភ្លែត ដើម្បីអរគុណ។

59
00:02:34,576 --> 00:02:37,662
អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក ហើយអរគុណខ្លួនឯង

60
00:02:37,712 --> 00:02:39,950
ដែលបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

61
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,644
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

62
00:02:41,694 --> 00:02:42,853
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ស។

63
00:02:42,903 --> 00:02:45,434
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 9 ដល់ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក។

64
00:02:45,484 --> 00:02:47,208
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

65
00:02:47,258 --> 00:02:49,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 4 លេខ 14 និងលេខ 445។

66
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

67
00:02:52,308 --> 00:02:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

68
00:02:54,423 --> 00:02:56,232

69
00:02:56,282 --> 00:02:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

70
00:02:58,590 --> 00:02:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's and the animals' pictures added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

Premium 3D animated fantasy style inspired by classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films, golden zodiac characters with polished gold textures, engraved ornaments and pink-and-gold accents, oversized sparkling eyes and very expressive faces. Background: a softly glowing Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under a huge full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, lotus pond, magical golden sparkles. Bright, high-contrast, saturated colors that stand out on a small phone screen; strong rim light separating the characters from the background; slightly blurred background so the faces pop. Big, simple composition with only two or three subjects. Keep the bottom-right corner free (YouTube puts the video length there). No watermark, no logo, no YouTube buttons or arrows. Twelve cute golden zodiac animals in a tight arc across the frame, all looking at the camera with excited faces; three of them shine much brighter with golden auras, one rests calmly in a soft blue bubble of light. Large bold text, at most a few words, in thick rounded sans-serif capitals with a dark outline and a soft drop shadow, perfectly spelled: "ALL 12 SIGNS" and "TODAY". The text sits across the top of the frame in the dark sky, never over a face.

### Subtitles – English (2026-10-30-long.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:03,243
Three signs are lucky today

2
00:00:03,293 --> 00:00:06,657
and one should take it easy.

3
00:00:06,707 --> 00:00:09,950
Where does your sign stand?

4
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,331
Today, Friday, 30 October 2026

5
00:00:12,381 --> 00:00:13,363
is an Ox day.

6
00:00:13,413 --> 00:00:16,140
It is a Great Day for everyone born

7
00:00:16,190 --> 00:00:17,887
in the Year of the Ox.

8
00:00:17,937 --> 00:00:19,950
This is your day to shine!

9
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:23,578
Steady and strong, the Ox symbolizes hard

10
00:00:23,628 --> 00:00:26,764
work, patience, and honest strength.

11
00:00:26,814 --> 00:00:29,950
Today, this good energy is with you.

12
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,814
Today's luckiest signs

13
00:00:31,864 --> 00:00:34,357
are the Ox, Snake and Rooster.

14
00:00:34,407 --> 00:00:36,475
Goat, take it easy today.

15
00:00:36,525 --> 00:00:39,950
Find your own sign in the chapters below.

16
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:41,162
Year of the Rat.

17
00:00:41,212 --> 00:00:44,268
Today is an ordinary, steady day for you.

18
00:00:44,318 --> 00:00:47,298
Your lucky color is green, and your best

19
00:00:47,348 --> 00:00:49,950
time is from 7 to 9 in the morning.

20
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,029
A steady, normal day.

21
00:00:52,079 --> 00:00:53,613
Keep a calm pace

22
00:00:53,663 --> 00:00:56,188
and finish what you start.

23
00:00:56,238 --> 00:00:59,950
Your lucky direction is the southeast.

24
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,200

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden ox find the lost seed before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the lotus's magic seed was gone. Without it, the lotus could not bloom, and the festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden ox. Today was an Ox day, and the ox felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A little bird named Dara flew down. "Little Ox," she said, "only you can find the lost seed. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Steady and strong, the Ox symbolizes hard work, patience, and honest strength. Dara smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To bring back the lotus seed, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the ox, and it set off on its adventure, with Dara right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Ox are reliable, honest, and hard-working. Oxen build success slowly and steadily through persistence.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was white, the ox's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The ox tied a white ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Dara, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the southeast, the ox's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But on the path stood an old, shaky bridge. The ox stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Ox can be stubborn and slow to adapt to sudden change.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Dara came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," she said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the ox crossed the bridge. With every step, the planks glowed gold. On the other side, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Dara. For the Ox today, that is from 9 to 11 in the morning. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the ox rang the golden bells 4 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The ox climbed 14 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found the magic lotus seed, glowing like a tiny sun. It was the heart of the giant lotus!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the seed, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Wednesday and Friday shone brightest, the Ox's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the seed carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Dara right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The ox placed the seed in the giant lotus. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, whoosh! The lotus opened in a burst of golden light!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The giant lotus bloomed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The ox felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Ox, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: white. Best time: from 9 to 11 in the morning. Lucky direction: southeast. Lucky numbers: 4, 14, and 445.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Dara fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
គោដាក់គ្រាប់ឈូក ចូលក្នុងផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ។ មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។ បន្ទាប់មក វឺត! ផ្កាឈូករីក ដោយពន្លឺមាសចែងចាំង!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ រីកម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ គោមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ស។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំបួន ដល់ម៉ោងដប់មួយ ព្រឹក។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខបួន លេខដប់បួន និងលេខបួនរយសែសិបប្រាំ។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងតារា ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

តើគោមាសតូចមួយ អាចរកគ្រាប់ឈូកដែលបាត់

2
00:00:02,574 --> 00:00:04,435
មុនព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់រះ បានទេ?

3
00:00:04,485 --> 00:00:07,082
កាលពីព្រេងនាយ នៅក្បែរប្រាសាទបុរាណមួយ

4
00:00:07,132 --> 00:00:09,950
ក្នុងប្រទេសកម្ពុជា រឿងរបស់យើងចាប់ផ្ដើម។

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,277
ប៉ុន្តែព្រឹកនេះ

6
00:00:11,327 --> 00:00:14,198
គ្រាប់វេទមន្តរបស់ផ្កាឈូក បានបាត់។

7
00:00:14,248 --> 00:00:16,764
បើគ្មានវា ផ្កាឈូកមិនអាចរីកបាន

8
00:00:16,814 --> 00:00:19,950
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យក៏មិនអាចចាប់ផ្ដើមបានដែរ។

9
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,998
នៅកណ្ដាលស្រះ ផ្កាឈូកមាសមួយបានរីក

10
00:00:23,048 --> 00:00:25,283
ហើយមានគោមាសតូចមួយ ចេញមក។

11
00:00:25,333 --> 00:00:27,474
ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ

12
00:00:27,524 --> 00:00:29,950
ហើយគោមានអារម្មណ៍ពិសេសណាស់។

13
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,526
កូនបក្សីតូចមួយ ឈ្មោះតារា ហោះចុះមក។

14
00:00:32,576 --> 00:00:35,026
តារានិយាយថា "គោតូចអើយ មានតែអ្នកទេ

15
00:00:35,076 --> 00:00:36,920
ដែលអាចរកគ្រាប់ឈូកដែលបាត់។

16
00:00:36,970 --> 00:00:39,041
ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់អ្នក!

17
00:00:39,091 --> 00:00:39,950
"

18
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:41,892
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ នឹងនរ និងរឹងមាំ។

19
00:00:41,942 --> 00:00:43,763
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវតំណាងឲ្យការខិតខំ

20
00:00:43,813 --> 00:00:46,281
ការអត់ធ្មត់ និងកម្លាំងដ៏ស្មោះត្រង់។

21
00:00:46,331 --> 00:00:47,144
តារាញញឹម។

22
00:00:47,194 --> 00:00:49,950
នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

23
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,879
ដើម្បីនាំគ្រាប់ឈូកត្រឡប់មកវិញ

24
00:00:52,929 --> 00:00:56,718
វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖

25
00:00:56,768 --> 00:00:59,950
ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

26
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,247
គោនិយាយថា "តោះទៅ!

27
00:01:02,297 --> 00:01:05,761
" ហើយវាក៏ចេញដំណើរផ្សងព្រេង

28
00:01:05,811 --> 00:01:09,950
ដោយមានតារា នៅក្បែរខ្លួនជានិច្ច។

29
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:13,314
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ជាមនុស្សទុកចិត្តបាន

30
00:01:13,364 --> 00:01:15,950
ស្មោះត្រង់ និងឧស្សាហ៍ព្យាយាម។

31
00:01:16,000 --> 00:01:19,950
ឆ្លូវសាងជោគជ័យបន្តិចម្តងៗដោយការតស៊ូមិនឈប់ឈរ។

32
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,323
រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ

33
00:01:22,373 --> 00:01:24,357
នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។

34
00:01:24,407 --> 00:01:26,899
មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។

35
00:01:26,949 --> 00:01:29,950
វាគឺពណ៌ស ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់គោថ្ងៃនេះ។

36
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,297
គោចងខ្សែបូពណ៌ស ជុំវិញក។

37
00:01:32,347 --> 00:01:36,174
តារានិយាយថា "ស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក

38
00:01:36,224 --> 00:01:38,726
ហើយសំណាងល្អ នឹងនៅជិតអ្នក។

39
00:01:38,776 --> 00:01:39,950
"

40
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,771
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ

41
00:01:42,821 --> 00:01:44,394
ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។

42
00:01:44,444 --> 00:01:46,360
ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

43
00:01:46,410 --> 00:01:49,950
វាចង្អុលទៅទិសអាគ្នេយ៍ ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់គោ។

44
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:53,235
ប៉ុន្តែនៅលើផ្លូវនោះ មានស្ពានចាស់មួយ ដែលរង្គើ។

45
00:01:53,285 --> 00:01:54,111
គោឈប់។

46
00:01:54,161 --> 00:01:56,300
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ជួនកាលរឹងរូស

47
00:01:56,350 --> 00:01:59,950
និងយឺតក្នុងការសម្របខ្លួនទៅនឹងការផ្លាស់ប្តូរភ្លាមៗ។

48
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:01,715
តារាមកជិត ហើយញញឹម។

49
00:02:01,765 --> 00:02:04,264
តារានិយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។

50
00:02:04,314 --> 00:02:06,028
ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។

51
00:02:06,078 --> 00:02:08,774
អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។

52
00:02:08,824 --> 00:02:09,950
"

53
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:13,359
ម្ដងមួយជំហានៗ គោឆ្លងកាត់ស្ពាន។

54
00:02:13,409 --> 00:02:16,882
រាល់ជំហាន ក្ដារស្ពានភ្លឺពណ៌មាស។

55
00:02:16,932 --> 00:02:19,950
នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

56
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,371
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា

57
00:02:23,421 --> 00:02:26,003
តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។

58
00:02:26,053 --> 00:02:29,950
តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

59
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,950
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ

60
00:02:33,000 --> 00:02:34,314
នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។

61
00:02:34,364 --> 00:02:37,223
នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង

62
00:02:37,273 --> 00:02:39,950
ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

63
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,644
តារានិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ

64
00:02:41,694 --> 00:02:44,466
បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។

65
00:02:44,516 --> 00:02:48,095
" សម្រាប់គោថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោង 9 ដល់ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក។

66
00:02:48,145 --> 00:02:49,950
ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

67
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:53,863
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង គោវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស ចំនួន 4 ដង

68
00:02:53,913 --> 00:02:56,580
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។

69
00:02:56,630 --> 00:02:57,885
ទីង!

70
00:02:57,935 --> 00:02:59,950
ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

71
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,314
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

72
00:03:03,364 --> 00:03:05,859
គោឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 14 កាំ

73
00:03:05,909 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

74
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:14,644
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគ្រាប់ឈូកវេទមន្ត

75
00:03:14,694 --> 00:03:17,195
ភ្លឺដូចព្រះអាទិត្យតូចមួយ។

76
00:03:17,245 --> 00:03:19,950
វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ!

77
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:21,122
នៅជុំវិញគ្រាប់ឈូក

78
00:03:21,172 --> 00:03:23,605
មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

79
00:03:23,655 --> 00:03:25,881
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

80
00:03:25,931 --> 00:03:27,950
ថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសុក្រ ភ្លឺជាងគេ

81
00:03:28,000 --> 00:03:29,950
ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ។

82
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,600
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

83
00:03:31,650 --> 00:03:35,581
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គ្រាប់ឈូកយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

84
00:03:35,631 --> 00:03:39,950
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានតារា នៅក្បែរ។

85
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

86
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

87
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

88
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,283
គោដាក់គ្រាប់ឈូក ចូលក្នុងផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ។

89
00:03:53,333 --> 00:03:55,876
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

90
00:03:55,926 --> 00:03:57,172
បន្ទាប់មក វឺត!

91
00:03:57,222 --> 00:03:59,950
ផ្កាឈូករីក ដោយពន្លឺមាសចែងចាំង!

92
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:01,968
ផ្កាឈូកដ៏ធំ រីកម្ដងទៀត។

93
00:04:02,018 --> 00:04:03,196
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

94
00:04:03,246 --> 00:04:06,617
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

95
00:04:06,667 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

96
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,173
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

97
00:04:13,223 --> 00:04:15,074
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

98
00:04:15,124 --> 00:04:17,801
គោមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

99
00:04:17,851 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

100
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:22,258
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ថ្ងៃនេះ

101
00:04:22,308 --> 00:04:23,967
ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

102
00:04:24,017 --> 00:04:26,617
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

103
00:04:26,667 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

104
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,644
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

105
00:04:31,694 --> 00:04:32,853
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ស។

106
00:04:32,903 --> 00:04:35,434
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 9 ដល់ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក។

107
00:04:35,484 --> 00:04:37,208
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

108
00:04:37,258 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 4 លេខ 14 និងលេខ 445។

109
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,107
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

110
00:04:44,157 --> 00:04:47,478
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងតារា ក៏លង់លក់

111
00:04:47,528 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

112
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

113
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

114
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

115
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

116
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

### Subtitles – English (2026-10-30-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,626
Can one little golden ox find the lost

2
00:00:02,676 --> 00:00:04,880
seed before the full moon rises?

3
00:00:04,930 --> 00:00:07,837
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:07,887 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,306
But this morning

6
00:00:11,356 --> 00:00:14,018
the lotus's magic seed was gone.

7
00:00:14,068 --> 00:00:17,153
Without it, the lotus could not bloom

8
00:00:17,203 --> 00:00:19,950
and the festival could not begin.

9
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:23,010
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus

10
00:00:23,060 --> 00:00:25,995
opened, and out came a little golden ox.

11
00:00:26,045 --> 00:00:27,413
Today was an Ox day

12
00:00:27,463 --> 00:00:29,950
and the ox felt something special.

13
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:33,020
A little bird named Dara flew down.

14
00:00:33,070 --> 00:00:34,862
"Little Ox," she said

15
00:00:34,912 --> 00:00:37,757
"only you can find the lost seed.

16
00:00:37,807 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

17
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:43,056
Steady and strong, the Ox symbolizes hard

18
00:00:43,106 --> 00:00:45,783
work, patience, and honest strength.

19
00:00:45,833 --> 00:00:46,692
Dara smiled.

20
00:00:46,742 --> 00:00:48,208
That was exactly why

21
00:00:48,258 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

22
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,172
To bring back the lotus seed

23
00:00:52,222 --> 00:00:54,394
the little hero needed three

24
00:00:54,444 --> 00:00:56,934
lucky treasures: its lucky color

25
00:00:56,984 --> 00:00:59,950
its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

26
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,755
"Let's go!" said the ox

27
00:01:02,805 --> 00:01:06,535
and it set off on its adventure

28
00:01:06,585 --> 00:01:09,950
with Dara right by its side.

29
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,488
People born in the Year of the Ox

30
00:01:12,538 --> 00:01:15,488
are reliable, honest, and hard-working.

31
00:01:15,538 --> 00:01:17,412
Oxen build success slowly

32
00:01:17,462 --> 00:01:19,950
and steadily through persistence.

33
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,240
The first treasure was waiting

34
00:01:22,290 --> 00:01:24,912
in a garden of a thousand lanterns.

35
00:01:24,962 --> 00:01:26,820
Only one shone brightest.

36
00:01:26,870 --> 00:01:29,950
It was white, the ox's lucky color today.

37
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,662
The ox tied a white

38
00:01:31,712 --> 00:01:33,734
ribbon around its neck.

39
00:01:33,784 --> 00:01:36,797
"Wear your lucky color," said Dara

40
00:01:36,847 --> 00:01:39,950
"and good luck stays close to you."

41
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,491
Next, they came to a crossroads

42
00:01:42,541 --> 00:01:43,802
with many paths.

43
00:01:43,852 --> 00:01:45,688
One path began to glow.

44
00:01:45,738 --> 00:01:47,901
It pointed to the southeast

45
00:01:47,951 --> 00:01:49,950
the ox's lucky direction.

46
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,341
But on the path stood

47
00:01:51,391 --> 00:01:52,731
an old, shaky bridge.

48
00:01:52,781 --> 00:01:53,725
The ox stopped.

49
00:01:53,775 --> 00:01:55,182
Sometimes, people born

50
00:01:55,232 --> 00:01:57,632
in the Year of the Ox can be stubborn

51
00:01:57,682 --> 00:01:59,950
and slow to adapt to sudden change.

52
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,521
Dara came close and smiled.

53
00:02:02,571 --> 00:02:05,093
"Breathe slowly," she said.

54
00:02:05,143 --> 00:02:06,807
"Trust your heart.

55
00:02:06,857 --> 00:02:09,950
You are stronger than you think."

56
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:13,311
Step by step, the ox crossed the bridge.

57
00:02:13,361 --> 00:02:16,673
With every step, the planks glowed gold.

58
00:02:16,723 --> 00:02:19,950
On the other side, it laughed with joy.

59
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,311
The little hero learned that its worries

60
00:02:23,361 --> 00:02:26,589
were small, and its good heart was big.

61
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

62
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

63
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413
stone door in the temple.

64
00:02:34,463 --> 00:02:36,562
Carved above it were bells

65
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950
stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

66
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,563
"The door opens only

67
00:02:41,613 --> 00:02:43,982
at your best time," said Dara.

68
00:02:44,032 --> 00:02:45,273
For the Ox today

69
00:02:45,323 --> 00:02:48,176
that is from 9 to 11 in the morning.

70
00:02:48,226 --> 00:02:49,950
They waited patiently.

71
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:53,571
When the time came, the ox rang the golden

72
00:02:53,621 --> 00:02:56,847
bells 4 times, its first lucky number.

73
00:02:56,897 --> 00:02:57,881
Ding!

74
00:02:57,931 --> 00:02:59,950
The door began to shine.

75
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,113
The door opened

76
00:03:01,163 --> 00:03:03,671
and a stairway of light appeared.

77
00:03:03,721 --> 00:03:06,074
The ox climbed 14 glowing steps

78
00:03:06,124 --> 00:03:07,857
its second lucky number

79
00:03:07,907 --> 00:03:09,950
up to the top of the tower.

80
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,968
At the top of the tower

81
00:03:12,018 --> 00:03:14,687
they found the magic lotus seed

82
00:03:14,737 --> 00:03:16,792
glowing like a tiny sun.

83
00:03:16,842 --> 00:03:19,950
It was the heart of the giant lotus!

84
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,698
Around the seed, seven little lights

85
00:03:22,748 --> 00:03:25,675
twinkled, one for each day of the week.

86
00:03:25,725 --> 00:03:28,423
Wednesday and Friday shone brightest

87
00:03:28,473 --> 00:03:29,950
the Ox's lucky days.

88
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:32,029
Time was running out.

89
00:03:32,079 --> 00:03:34,603
Holding the seed carefully

90
00:03:34,653 --> 00:03:37,376
the little hero hurried home

91
00:03:37,426 --> 00:03:39,950
with Dara right beside it.

92
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

93
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

94
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

95
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

96
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,206
The ox placed the seed in the giant lotus.

97
00:03:53,256 --> 00:03:55,609
For a moment, nothing happened.

98
00:03:55,659 --> 00:03:56,617
Then, whoosh!

99
00:03:56,667 --> 00:03:57,857
The lotus opened

100
00:03:57,907 --> 00:03:59,950
in a burst of golden light!

101
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,223
The giant lotus bloomed again.

102
00:04:02,273 --> 00:04:04,192
Lanterns rose into the sky

103
00:04:04,242 --> 00:04:06,692
and the full-moon festival began.

104
00:04:06,742 --> 00:04:07,905
Everyone cheered

105
00:04:07,955 --> 00:04:09,950
for the little golden hero!

106
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,743
The full moon shone down warmly

107
00:04:12,793 --> 00:04:14,815
as if to say thank you.

108
00:04:14,865 --> 00:04:17,608
The ox felt peaceful and proud.

109
00:04:17,658 --> 00:04:19,950
It had done its very best.

110
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,143
If you were born in the Year of the Ox

111
00:04:23,193 --> 00:04:24,992
today is your day too.

112
00:04:25,042 --> 00:04:27,681
Like our hero, be brave, be kind

113
00:04:27,731 --> 00:04:29,950
and follow your good heart.

114
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,908
Let's remember today's luck.

115
00:04:31,958 --> 00:04:33,237
Lucky color: white.

116
00:04:33,287 --> 00:04:35,964
Best time: from 9 to 11 in the morning.

117
00:04:36,014 --> 00:04:37,852
Lucky direction: southeast.

118
00:04:37,902 --> 00:04:39,950
Lucky numbers: 4, 14, and 445.

119
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,219
And so, under the bright full moon

120
00:04:43,269 --> 00:04:46,681
the little golden hero and Dara fell

121
00:04:46,731 --> 00:04:49,950
fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

122
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:51,617
That's your Chinese zodiac

123
00:04:51,667 --> 00:04:52,771
fortune for today.

124
00:04:52,821 --> 00:04:54,053
See all twelve signs

125
00:04:54,103 --> 00:04:56,745
and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign.com.

126
00:04:56,795 --> 00:04:58,860
Follow us for tomorrow's fortune.

127
00:04:58,910 --> 00:04:59,950
Have a lucky day!

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Goat (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Goat, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Goat clashes with the Ox day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Goat as ingredients (the Goat is the only animal in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the goat hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little lotus lantern keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Stop the scroll
   English: Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Goat? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.

   English: Year of the Goat, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.

   English: Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Goat, here's why.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ អ្វីៗហាក់ដូចជាមិនសូវស្រួលមែនទេ? បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ នេះជាមូលហេតុ។
4. Send it to a friend
   English: Know someone born in the Year of the Goat? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.

5. Only one sign
   English: Out of all twelve signs, only one should slow down today: the Goat.

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)
Chanda, a friendly young Cambodian woman presenter with big warm amber-brown eyes, rosy cheeks and a bright open smile, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun with a golden lotus hairpin and dangling gold chains, small gold flower earrings, a cream-and-gold brocade Khmer blouse with a high collar, puffed shoulders and three-quarter sleeves, a gold chain necklace with a large round golden medallion showing the twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a flower buckle and a red gem, and a deep navy sampot skirt woven with gold Khmer diamond patterns and a front pleated panel, stands on the carved stone temple terrace above the moonlit lotus pond, facing the camera and speaking warmly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures. She starts by raising one open hand toward the camera like a friendly "stop" sign, with a caring, slightly urgent expression, then lowers it and keeps talking warmly. Below her, at the edge of the pond where a path of flat stepping stones leads up to the temple, an adorable golden goat with big soft eyes, curled shining horns and a tiny bell sits looking a little tired and lets out a quiet sigh with its mouth closed; it does not speak. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward Chanda. Chanda matches her reference image; the goat matches its reference image. Chanda looks exactly like her reference picture: same face, hairstyle, outfit and jewelry in every scene.

## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Goat? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 30 October, is an Ox day, opposite the Goat in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be indecisive or overly dependent on others. Pause before you react.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

## Long YouTube video – full fortune, 8:40 (mid-roll ads)
A full daily fortune for YouTube (8:40, 52 clips of 10 seconds), long enough for mid-roll ads. In order: the hook, the Great Day sign (Ox) in depth, the two Good Day signs (Snake and Rooster), the sign to take it easy (Goat) with its little story, the other eight signs, then a recap and goodbye. Add Chanda and the 12 character pictures as ingredients in Flow (each part uses its own animal). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 4 and 52; in the others she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. Set the clip length to 10 seconds, make the clips in order 1 to 52, and paste the chapters into the YouTube description.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Great Day: Ox
2:40 Good Day: Snake
3:30 Good Day: Rooster
4:20 Take it easy: Goat
5:20 All the other signs
8:00 Recap and goodbye

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Every sign in one line
   English: One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានមួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ តើអ្នកជាឆ្នាំណា?
2. Before a big decision
   English: Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down.
   Khmer: មុននឹងសម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ពេលល្អ និងថាតើគួរធ្វើយឺតៗឬទេ។
3. Speak to the careful sign
   English: Born in the Year of the Goat? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែមែនទេ? សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ មុនធ្វើរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកផ្សេងទៀត សំណាងរបស់អ្នកក៏នៅទីនេះដែរ។
4. Name the winner
   English: Today the Ox has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ហើយមានមួយឆ្នាំគួរប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ តើជាឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
5. Comment first
   English: Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.
   Khmer: សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នកឥឡូវនេះ។ មុនវីដេអូនេះចប់ អ្នកនឹងដឹងពណ៌ ពេល និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video. Why: in one line it gives every viewer a stake (a Great Day, a Good Day, or a day to slow down) and ends on a question, so people stay to find their own sign. To use another hook, replace the voice line in scene 1.

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's and the animals' pictures added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

Background: a softly glowing Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under a huge full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, lotus pond, magical golden sparkles. ការអានរាសីចិនតាមប្រពៃណី សម្រាប់ការកម្សាន្ត និងការឆ្លុះបញ្ចាំងខ្លួនប៉ុណ្ណោះ មិនមែនជាការព្យាករណ៍វិទ្យាសាស្ត្រទេ។

#ជោគជតារាសី #រាសីប្រចាំថ្ងៃ #ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ #ផ្កាយកំណើត

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)
Chanda, a friendly young Cambodian woman presenter with big warm amber-brown eyes, rosy cheeks and a bright open smile, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun with a golden lotus hairpin and dangling gold chains, small gold flower earrings, a cream-and-gold brocade Khmer blouse with a high collar, puffed shoulders and three-quarter sleeves, a gold chain necklace with a large round golden medallion showing the twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a flower buckle and a red gem, and a deep navy sampot skirt woven with gold Khmer diamond patterns and a front pleated panel, stands on the moonlit temple terrace beside the lotus pond, facing the camera, smiling warmly and speaking directly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures. Behind her, twelve cute golden zodiac animals sit on glowing lotus pedestals in a half circle: the ox shines brightest with a golden aura, and the goat rests calmly inside a soft bubble of golden light. The animals move gently and look around curiously, mouths closed. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward her face. Chanda looks exactly like her reference picture: same face, hairstyle, outfit and jewelry in every scene.
English voice: Audio: Chanda's voice, identical in every clip of this series (the same woman, same timbre, same pitch, same accent, same speed, same energy): a young Cambodian woman in her mid-twenties speaking clear, natural English with a neutral accent, medium-high pitch, a soft, warm and bright tone, a gentle smile in her voice, the same calm, cheerful energy from the first word to the last (never louder, faster, deeper, older or more dramatic), a steady pace of about 150 words per minute, even volume, recorded close to a good studio microphone in a quiet room with no echo or reverb; no whispering, no singing, no other voices. Chanda is on camera and speaks to the viewer with natural lip movements that match her words. She says: "One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?" Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak.

### Scene 5 – The Ox wakes up (0:40–0:50)
Extreme close-up of a closed pink lotus on the moonlit pond. It opens petal by petal and reveals a strong, adorable golden ox with big gentle eyes, polished curved horns and ruby studs on its harness curled up asleep on its golden pedestal. It wakes, blinks its big sparkling eyes, stretches its whole body and looks around with delight. Smooth pull-back from the close-up to a full-body shot. The ox is fully animated and alive, never still like a statue: it breathes slowly and steadily, nods its big head, flicks its ears, swishes its tail and takes strong, calm steps. ## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today is an Ox day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Ox, Snake and Rooster are lucky today, and the Goat should slow down. Find your sign in the chapters below.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Before we start, comment your sign below, and subscribe so you get your fortune every day.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Friday, 30 October: a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Ox!

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Steady and strong, the Ox symbolizes hard work, patience, and honest strength.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
People born in the Year of the Ox are often described as reliable, honest, and hard-working.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Your luck is strong today. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
For love today, be warm and kind. A kind word or a small gift brings you closer.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
For work and study, take one clear step at a time, and finish what matters most first.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
For money, keep it simple. Plan before you spend, and save a little for tomorrow.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
Your lucky color today is white. Wear it, or carry something white with you.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Your best time is from 9 to 11 in the morning, and your lucky direction is the southeast.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Your lucky numbers: 4, 14, and 445. Comment your sign below, and save this video!

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The Ox's lucky days of the week are Wednesday and Friday. Keep them for plans that matter.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
A gentle reminder: Ox people can be stubborn and slow to adapt to sudden change. Stay balanced today.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Next, the Year of the Snake: a Good Day! The Snake shares a lucky triangle with the Ox.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
Team up with a friend today. Shared plans go well.

Your lucky color is red, and your best time is from 1 to 3 in the early morning.

Snake friends, stay close to the Ox people today. Good luck grows when you share it.

Your lucky color is yellow, and your best time is from 1 to 3 in the early morning.

Rooster friends, stay close to the Ox people today. Good luck grows when you share it.

Today, 30 October, is an Ox day, opposite the Goat in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next.

Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:53,018
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ នឹងនរ និងរឹងមាំ។

17
00:00:53,068 --> 00:00:55,973
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវតំណាងឲ្យការខិតខំ

18
00:00:56,023 --> 00:00:59,950
ការអត់ធ្មត់ និងកម្លាំងដ៏ស្មោះត្រង់។

19
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:05,069
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ជារឿយៗ ត្រូវបានគេស្គាល់ថា

20
00:01:05,119 --> 00:01:09,950
ទុកចិត្តបាន ស្មោះត្រង់ និងឧស្សាហ៍ព្យាយាម។

21
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:14,379
ថ្ងៃនេះ សំណាងរបស់អ្នកល្អខ្លាំង។

22
00:01:14,429 --> 00:01:19,950
សូមទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ ហើយចែករំលែកភាពរីករាយ។

23
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:24,717
សម្រាប់ស្នេហា ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមានចិត្តកក់ក្ដៅ។

24
00:01:24,767 --> 00:01:29,950
ពាក្យល្អ ឬអំណោយតូចមួយ នាំឲ្យកាន់តែជិតស្និទ្ធ។

25
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:33,664
សម្រាប់ការងារ និងការសិក្សា

26
00:01:33,714 --> 00:01:39,950
សូមធ្វើម្ដងមួយជំហាន ហើយបញ្ចប់រឿងសំខាន់មុនគេ។

27
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:43,924
សម្រាប់លុយកាក់ សូមចាយឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

28
00:01:43,974 --> 00:01:47,771
គិតមុននឹងចាយ ហើយសន្សំទុកបន្តិច

29
00:01:47,821 --> 00:01:49,950
សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃស្អែក។

30
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:54,209
ពណ៌សំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌ស។

31
00:01:54,259 --> 00:01:59,950
សូមស្លៀក ឬយករបស់ពណ៌ស ជាប់ខ្លួន។

32
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:05,756
ពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 9 ដល់ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក

33
00:02:05,806 --> 00:02:09,950
ហើយទិសសំណាង គឺទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

34
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,899
លេខសំណាង៖ 4 14 និង 445។

35
00:02:12,949 --> 00:02:17,258
សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងមតិយោបល់

36
00:02:17,308 --> 00:02:19,950
ហើយរក្សាទុកវីដេអូនេះ!

37
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,101
ថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ

38
00:02:23,151 --> 00:02:26,251
គឺថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសុក្រ។

39
00:02:26,301 --> 00:02:29,950
សូមទុកសម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ។

40
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:33,859
រំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ជួនកាលរឹងរូស

41
00:02:33,909 --> 00:02:38,405
និងយឺតក្នុងការសម្របខ្លួនទៅនឹងការផ្លាស់ប្តូរភ្លាមៗ។

42
00:02:38,455 --> 00:02:39,950
សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព។

43
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:43,621
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ!

44
00:02:43,671 --> 00:02:47,925
ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង

45
00:02:47,975 --> 00:02:49,950
ជាមួយឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ។

46
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:54,771
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមសហការជាមួយមិត្ត។

47
00:02:54,821 --> 00:02:59,950
ផែនការរួមគ្នា នឹងដំណើរការល្អ។

48
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,001
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ក្រហម

49
00:03:03,051 --> 00:03:09,950
ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

50
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:14,340
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសឦសាន។

51
00:03:14,390 --> 00:03:19,950
លេខសំណាង៖ 8 82 និង 897។

52
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:23,160
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ថ្ងៃនេះ

53
00:03:23,210 --> 00:03:26,370
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ។

54
00:03:26,420 --> 00:03:29,950
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

55
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:33,512
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំរកា៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ!

56
00:03:33,562 --> 00:03:37,758
ឆ្នាំរកា ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង

57
00:03:37,808 --> 00:03:39,950
ជាមួយឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ។

58
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,104
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស

59
00:03:44,154 --> 00:03:47,181
និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។

60
00:03:47,231 --> 00:03:49,950
សូមរក្សាស្នាមញញឹម។

61
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:52,757
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌លឿង

62
00:03:52,807 --> 00:03:59,950
ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

63
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:04,340
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសឦសាន។

64
00:04:04,390 --> 00:04:09,950
លេខសំណាង៖ 8 88 និង 586។

65
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,899
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា ថ្ងៃនេះ

66
00:04:12,949 --> 00:04:16,232
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ។

67
00:04:16,282 --> 00:04:19,950
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

68
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,711
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទី 30 ខែតុលា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ

69
00:04:23,761 --> 00:04:26,556
ទល់មុខឆ្នាំមមែ ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។

70
00:04:26,606 --> 00:04:29,950
មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

71
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,090
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។

72
00:04:31,140 --> 00:04:34,248
មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។

73
00:04:34,298 --> 00:04:37,494
ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ

74
00:04:37,544 --> 00:04:39,950
គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

75
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:41,777
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖

76
00:04:41,827 --> 00:04:45,238
ជួនកាលស្ទាក់ស្ទើរក្នុងការសម្រេចចិត្ត

77
00:04:45,288 --> 00:04:47,354
ឬពឹងផ្អែកលើអ្នកដទៃពេក។

78
00:04:47,404 --> 00:04:49,950
សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។

79
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:54,394
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់

80
00:04:54,444 --> 00:04:56,370
និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។

81
00:04:56,420 --> 00:04:59,950
ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

82
00:05:00,000 --> 00:05:02,422
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់

83
00:05:02,472 --> 00:05:06,130
សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។

84
00:05:06,180 --> 00:05:09,950
បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។

85
00:05:10,000 --> 00:05:12,705
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។

86
00:05:12,755 --> 00:05:17,093
រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ

87
00:05:17,143 --> 00:05:19,950
ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

88
00:05:20,000 --> 00:05:22,950
ឆ្នាំជូត៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

89
00:05:23,000 --> 00:05:25,521
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌បៃតង។

90
00:05:25,571 --> 00:05:29,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 ព្រឹក។

91
00:05:30,000 --> 00:05:32,242
ជាថ្ងៃធម្មតា និងនឹងនរ។

92
00:05:32,292 --> 00:05:34,221
សូមធ្វើការដោយស្ងប់ៗ

93
00:05:34,271 --> 00:05:37,658
ហើយបញ្ចប់អ្វីដែលអ្នកបានចាប់ផ្ដើម។

94
00:05:37,708 --> 00:05:39,950
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

95
00:05:40,000 --> 00:05:42,642
ឆ្នាំខាល៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

96
00:05:42,692 --> 00:05:45,206
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ប្រផេះ។

97
00:05:45,256 --> 00:05:49,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក ដល់ម៉ោង 1 រសៀល។

98
00:05:50,000 --> 00:05:53,398
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមធ្វើអ្វីៗឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

99
00:05:53,448 --> 00:05:57,421
ជំហានតូចៗ និងនឹងនរ ល្អជាងការលោតធំៗ។

100
00:05:57,471 --> 00:05:59,950
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងត្បូង។

101
00:06:00,000 --> 00:06:02,950
ឆ្នាំថោះ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

102
00:06:03,000 --> 00:06:05,664
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ស្វាយ។

103
00:06:05,714 --> 00:06:09,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល។

104
00:06:10,000 --> 00:06:13,376
ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ សម្រាប់រៀបចំផែនការ។

105
00:06:13,426 --> 00:06:15,691
សូមរៀបចំរបស់របរ គិតទុកមុន

106
00:06:15,741 --> 00:06:18,191
និងសម្រាកឲ្យបានល្អនៅយប់នេះ។

107
00:06:18,241 --> 00:06:19,950
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។

108
00:06:20,000 --> 00:06:22,713
ឆ្នាំរោង៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

109
00:06:22,763 --> 00:06:24,950
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។

110
00:06:25,000 --> 00:06:29,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 យប់ ដល់ម៉ោង 1 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

111
00:06:30,000 --> 00:06:32,290
ជាថ្ងៃធម្មតា និងនឹងនរ។

112
00:06:32,340 --> 00:06:34,312
សូមធ្វើការដោយស្ងប់ៗ

113
00:06:34,362 --> 00:06:37,822
ហើយបញ្ចប់អ្វីដែលអ្នកបានចាប់ផ្ដើម។

114
00:06:37,872 --> 00:06:39,950
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងជើង។

115
00:06:40,000 --> 00:06:42,827
ឆ្នាំមមី៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

116
00:06:42,877 --> 00:06:45,429
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ស្វាយ។

117
00:06:45,479 --> 00:06:49,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 3 ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

118
00:06:50,000 --> 00:06:53,564
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមធ្វើអ្វីៗឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

119
00:06:53,614 --> 00:06:57,781
ជំហានតូចៗ និងនឹងនរ ល្អជាងការលោតធំៗ។

120
00:06:57,831 --> 00:06:59,950
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

121
00:07:00,000 --> 00:07:02,617
ឆ្នាំវក៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

122
00:07:02,667 --> 00:07:04,883
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌មាស។

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Premium 3D animated fantasy film quality, inspired by classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films: cute and majestic golden zodiac characters with oversized sparkling eyes, adorable expressive faces, soft rounded features, polished gold textures with intricate engraved ornaments and delicate pink and gold accents, alive and emotionally expressive. Setting: a softly illuminated Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under a glowing full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, floating lotus flowers, ornate pink-and-gold lotus details, magical sparkles and warm golden reflections. Cinematic lighting, rich colors, soft atmospheric glow, realistic metallic reflections, beautiful depth of field, gentle slow camera movement, professional animated-movie rendering. ## Scene 1 – Ox day (Chanda on camera)
Chanda, a friendly young Cambodian woman presenter with big warm amber-brown eyes, rosy cheeks and a bright open smile, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun with a golden lotus hairpin and dangling gold chains, small gold flower earrings, a cream-and-gold brocade Khmer blouse with a high collar, puffed shoulders and three-quarter sleeves, a gold chain necklace with a large round golden medallion showing the twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a flower buckle and a red gem, and a deep navy sampot skirt woven with gold Khmer diamond patterns and a front pleated panel, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. Chanda matches her reference image. Then: magical and powerful. A strong, adorable golden ox with big gentle eyes, polished curved horns and ruby studs on its harness stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it stamps a hoof and a shockwave of golden light ripples across the pond, then it rises into the air on glowing golden clouds. The lotus pond ripples with golden light and lanterns rise into the sky. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. A strong, adorable golden ox with big gentle eyes, polished curved horns and ruby studs on its harness, a cute, elegant golden snake with big shiny eyes and jewel-like patterned scales and a proud, adorable golden rooster with big bright eyes, a ruby-red comb and shimmering tail feathers fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, a ring of golden energy explodes outward, lotus flowers bloom across the pond and a shower of sparkles and gold coins falls from the sky. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Goat, take it easy
Premium 3D animated golden style, same characters as the reference pictures. An adorable golden goat with big soft eyes, curled shining horns and a tiny bell curls up on a lotus pedestal as a soft protective bubble of golden light forms around it like a shield. Gentle glowing butterflies and petals circle it, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The goat matches the reference image.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Golden light beams shoot up from the temple towers into the night sky and burst into gentle golden fireworks shaped like lotus flowers. Hundreds of floating lanterns rise over the glowing pond and the huge full moon. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with twelve cute golden zodiac animals around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)

តើរាសីរបស់អ្នក ស្ថិតក្នុងចំណោមបីឆ្នាំ ដែលមានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះឬទេ? នេះគឺជោគជតារាសី សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃទីសាមសិប ខែតុលា ឆ្នាំពីរពាន់ម្ភៃប្រាំមួយ។
ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ។ ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ និងឆ្នាំរកា មានសំណាងល្អជាងគេនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ ចំណែកឆ្នាំមមែ គួរសម្រាកឲ្យស្រួលខ្លួន ធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ និងកុំប្រញាប់ក្នុងការសម្រេចចិត្ត។
សម្រាប់លេខសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវដប់បួន និងបួនរយសែសិបប្រាំ។ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ប៉ែតសិបពីរ និងប្រាំបីរយកៅសិបប្រាំពីរ។ ឆ្នាំរកាប៉ែតសិបប្រាំបី និងប្រាំរយប៉ែតសិបប្រាំមួយ។ សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយនិយាយម្ដងទៀត!
ចូលមើលលេខសំណាង និងលម្អិតសម្រាប់រាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ នៅ mybirthsign.com
